# FTD Theory Documentation Index

**Framework:** Foundational Ternary Dynamics
**Status:** Curated catalog. **Single source of truth for claim status: [`07_assessment/core_ledgers/LEDGER.md`](07_assessment/core_ledgers/LEDGER.md)**; if LEDGER and individual docs disagree, LEDGER wins.
**Single Source of Truth (spec):** [`docs/SPEC_FTD.md`](../SPEC_FTD.md)
**Foundational commitment:** undefined-boundary lattice ontology — see [`07_assessment/AUDIT_INFINITY_REFRAME.md`](07_assessment/framework_postulates_constitution/AUDIT_INFINITY_REFRAME.md) and [`07_assessment/campaigns/archive/reframe_deployment/CANONICAL_REFRAME.md`](07_assessment/campaigns/archive/reframe_deployment/CANONICAL_REFRAME.md).

---

## Document Organization

**Theory custodian agent:** [`AGENT_THEORY_CUSTODIAN.md`](AGENT_THEORY_CUSTODIAN.md) — Cursor skill `ftd-theory-custodian` + `scripts/theory/sync_theory_briefing.py` for session freshness.

This index organizes the curated theory catalog into 10 logical categories (9 subdirectories + this file).
Archived documents are in the `archive/` subdirectory.

> **Cleanup note:** Treat this file as a curated catalog, not a raw directory listing. **Every cluster maintains its own local navigation index** — see the *Local cluster indexes* table below; open a cluster's index for a categorised, tag-annotated inventory of its docs. Superseded or closed-negative documents should live under `archive/` or a local `archive/` subdirectory.

### Structural Guides

- [01_reference/SPEC_FQCR_V1_5_CHECKPOINT.md](01_reference/SPEC_FQCR_V1_5_CHECKPOINT.md) — Phase G/H Checkpoint (ontology shift)
- [META_STRUCTURE.md](META_STRUCTURE.md) — placement rules, archive rules, and category boundaries
- [07_assessment/core_ledgers/TRACKER_ONTIC_TRUTH.md](07_assessment/core_ledgers/TRACKER_ONTIC_TRUTH.md) — canonical bedrock tracker (5-tier truth ranking with verification artifacts)
- [../SPEC_FTD.md](../SPEC_FTD.md) — authoritative framework spec

| `archive/` | Historical and superseded theory docs |

## 12. By Physics Sector

> **(FTD-0147 [SYNTHESIS]).** This section presents an alternate axis of the catalog: every active theory doc grouped by physics sector. The 10-sector decomposition matches [`SPEC_OPEN_MATH_BY_SECTOR.md`](01_reference/SPEC_OPEN_MATH_BY_SECTOR.md) so a reader can switch between "all docs in sector X" and "what's still open in sector X" using the same mental map.
>
> **Conventions:** docs are listed under their **primary** sector. Cross-sector docs (full-stack specs, audits, trackers) are in §12.11. Filenames omit the `.md` extension. Directory prefix in parentheses indicates the canonical location (`01_` = 01_reference/, `02_` = 02_foundations/, etc.). Archived docs are not listed (see §11 + `archive/`).
